**Status: PARTIAL.** Initial execution pass complete: seven product defects found and fixed; full regression passed 14,056 tests across 459 files; all 5 final packaged native scenarios and installed Restricted Mode passed. Clean-machine/account-transition and required elapsed-time profiles remain unrun, so the full feature is not marked PASS.

Findings:

- **PC01-F01 (FIXED_REGRESSION_VERIFIED):** Delayed discovery could launch an installer after Skip, panel closure, or a newer choice. Per-panel cancellation now reaches manager stages and prevents that continuation.
- **PC01-F02 (FIXED_REGRESSION_VERIFIED):** Late authentication results could restart or replace polling after dismissal or a newer choice. Polls now belong to the current setup attempt and panel.
- **PC01-F03 (FIXED_REGRESSION_VERIFIED):** Hung discovery/auth checks could wait indefinitely. Setup probes and polling now have bounded waits, recoverable errors, and cached onboarding fallback.
- **PC01-F04 (FIXED_REGRESSION_VERIFIED):** Late credential prompts could persist credentials after dismissal, including OpenCode password deletion. Cancellation now closes prompts and guards manager, MiniMax, and OpenCode credential mutations.
- **PC01-T01 (TEST_FIXED):** Both initial native failures were test assumptions: selector matched a hidden badge, and New Chat was incorrectly required to delete unsent text. The harness now targets the action and verifies unique acknowledged conversation IDs while retaining drafts.
- **PC01-F05 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** MiniMax was missing from onboarding. Its card is restored; browser coverage checks all 16 registered providers and the native run reached its sign-in flow.
- **PC01-F06 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Completed provider-owned API prompts were ignored and polled after cancellation/failure/success. Their outcomes now complete immediately; external CLI login still polls. Native Escape returned recovery in 126 ms.
- **PC01-F07 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** The wizard covered the authentication dialog and intercepted Confirm clicks. The dialog now stacks above the wizard; actual browser and native clicks pass.

Still required:

- Real clean-machine no-CLI and one-CLI environments; zero-install account sign-in/cancellation, stale credentials, and actual installer/account transitions.
- Real login completion from another window and restart after each wizard stage; controlled fault injection does not substitute for those journeys.
- S (45–60 minutes), W (8 hours), P (five actual days) with specified operations and process/performance evidence remain NOT_RUN. Short native cycles do not satisfy elapsed-time profiles.

Recorder fix underway; independently reproduced product fixes remain valid. | [1](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/native-runtime.log), [2](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/native-runtime/observations.json) |
| PC02-A35 | VERIFIED_PACKAGE_NATIVE_UPDATE | PASS | All 4 native scenarios passed in 7 minutes against the verified package. Real diagnostics, 50 refreshes across three panels (62.204 seconds), and debug UI recovery passed. After closing the two test editor tabs, the actual sidebar Update button changed the selected disposable Codex installation from 0.159.3 to 0.162.0; refresh removed its update notice and preserved provider/model/draft. Update-to-diagnostics took 307.540 seconds. Actual Codex gpt-5.6-sol returned PC02_RETRY:100,200,1000 with no tools. Both old app-server PIDs were absent; two replacement PIDs belonged to the selected installation. This does not certify simultaneous multi-panel update continuity. | [1](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/native-verified.log), [2](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/native-verified/observations.json) |
| PC02-A36 | ORIGINAL_INSTALL_ISOLATION_AND_CLEANUP | PASS | Original /usr/local/bin/codex remains 0.153.4 with the same realpath and all 10 package-file hashes unchanged. Selected disposable shim and manifest both report 0.162.0. Native runner exited zero; no selected-prefix app-server or installer processes remain. git diff --check passes. | [1](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/global-codex-before.json), [2](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/global-codex-after.json), [3](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/codex-after-update.json), [4](../out-test/feature-tests/PC-02/native-verified.log) |

Findings:

- **PC02-F01 (FIXED):** Discovery refresh could reuse obsolete work, seed caches after disposal, or stall all providers behind one hung backend.
- **PC02-F02 (FIXED):** A provider discovery/auth failure could suppress the entire diagnostics report; reports omitted the selected executable path.
- **PC02-F03 (FIXED):** CLI updates could target a different PATH/global installation instead of the selected custom installation. Unknown or missing paths now produce manual instructions.
- **PC02-F04 (FIXED):** Late task creation or a throwing terminate call could leak an upgrade task or prevent settlement.
- **PC02-F05 (FIXED):** Model-triggered upgrades could continue after their panel, conversation or selection changed and report readiness into a replacement flow.
- **PC02-F06 (FIXED):** Diagnostic platform/runtime strings were interpreted as HTML; all diagnostic fields and manual update reasons now render as escaped text.
- **PC02-F07 (FIXED):** Unbounded or shell-interpreted discovery/prerequisite probes could stall setup, misread version noise, or use a different Node environment from the selected npm installer.
- **PC02-F08 (FIXED):** Update cards could advertise a newer version than Node allowed, and ordinary commands used latest instead of the advertised version. Payload now distinguishes versions and the command pins the advertised target. Browser and native validation passed.
- **PC02-F09 (FIXED):** Diagnostics mixed PATH npm metadata with selected NVM npm runtime. Version, prefix, writability and executable now describe the selected npm; independent probes run concurrently.
- **PC02-F10 (FIXED):** Refresh Detection updated provider availability but left stale CLI update notices. It now broadcasts the current update list to every panel, including an empty list when no upgrade remains.
- **PC02-F11 (FIXED):** Warmed processes reused the old CLI after a version/path/file change. Each panel now checks executable identity before the next turn and starts a new process when needed. Active responses finish, and native conversation IDs are preserved.
- **PC02-F12 (FIXED):** A backend absent from the startup snapshot could later be discovered without a registry update check. Refresh Detection now schedules enabled, coalesced background checks without blocking its acknowledgement. This gap was independently reproduced. The prior native timeout was a separate harness-listener defect, resolved in the final run.

Still required:

- Run every declared installer method in clean disposable macOS/Windows/Linux environments, including native Windows .cmd and minimum/current supported VS Code.
- Verify product-driven missing-backend install, fresh login and inference, plus an actual model-triggered newer-CLI upgrade and request continuation.
- Perform five actual cancelled/failed product installations followed by retries, covering terminal close, missing npm/Node, permissions, network interruption, noisy/truncated output and process cleanup. Controlled tests do not replace these real cases.
- Exercise an older executable earlier on PATH across selected-install upgrades and editor restarts; complete simultaneous native refresh/cancellation races.
- Complete actual S (45–60 minutes), W (8 hours with 250 useful operations and idle/sleep) and P (five days with nightly shutdown and day-three update), with resource, latency and process measurements. Fast refresh loops are not elapsed-time profiles.

Findings:

- **PC03-F01 (FIXED):** Setup status checks could throw or stall behind one provider, hiding healthy siblings and expiry guidance.
- **PC03-F02 (FIXED):** Shared API-key authentication prompts ignored setup cancellation.
- **PC03-F03 (FIXED):** Provider-owned credential changes did not invalidate cached authentication results on every outcome.
- **PC03-F04 (FIXED):** Auth failure followed by done could count as success, send queued work and lose interrupted output. Host now treats auth failure as terminal and preserves useful partial content.
- **PC03-F05 (FIXED):** Auth recovery cards lost detached-panel identity, allowed repeated launches/cards and could remain hidden after dismissal or leave partial output/tools streaming.
- **PC03-F06 (FIXED):** Terminal polling treated unchanged local credentials as verified sign-in. It now asks the user to retry inference to verify authentication.
- **PC03-F07 (FIXED):** Real Codex retry progress was parsed as terminal failure. Explicit willRetry notifications now remain nonterminal; real rerun reached the final auth failure without an extra Mysti dispatch.
- **PC03-F08 (FIXED):** Enriched spawn environment cached credentials indefinitely. Only PATH enrichment is now cached; each spawn receives current environment credentials.
- **PC03-F09 (FIXED):** Warmed persistent adapters retained old environment credentials. Private credential generations restart them on the next turn without interrupting active work.
- **PC03-F10 (FIXED):** Codex auth detection ignored an explicitly configured CODEX_HOME and reported credentials from a different profile.
- **PC03-F11 (FIXED):** Native auth failures could remain generic errors while filesystem permission/config messages were mistaken for login failures. Classification now distinguishes tested auth and non-auth cases.
- **PC03-F12 (FIXED):** Known credential values could appear in CLI error diagnostics or split stderr logging, including old credentials after rotation. Errors use current and issuing-process redaction before delivery/logging; raw fragment logging removed.

Still required:

- Execute every supported auth method from signed out through cancelled login, completed fresh login and inference using disposable test accounts.
- Revoke a test credential after turn 5; validate discovery, active stream, queued follow-up and idle rejection/recovery, invalid keys, valid keys without model entitlement, and two distinct accounts in isolated profiles. Current account restoration and synthetic keys do not certify these cases.
- Run the real all-provider healthy-sibling failure matrix, native Windows/Linux and minimum/current supported VS Code.
- Complete required S (45–60 minutes), E (24 hours / 750 useful operations / 20 fault events) and P (five actual days with overnight rotation) with measured resource, latency and process-cleanup gates. Short loops and the 37-second live journey do not satisfy elapsed-time profiles.

Findings:

- **PC04-D01 (FIXED):** Invalid, failed, or late catalogs could break discovery, overwrite newer endpoint data, or lose sibling/custom catalog edits. Registry normalizes rows, scopes generations, isolates persistence failures, and serializes cache updates; 77 targeted checks pass.
- **PC04-D02 (FIXED):** Late settings saves and custom-model dialogs could overwrite the current selection or a replacement conversation. Selection revisions and conversation ownership now guard application.
- **PC04-D03 (FIXED):** Sending immediately after model selection could still use the old configured override. Host waits for settings persistence before dispatch and checks stop/conversation ownership.
- **PC04-D04 (FIXED):** Effective-model labels diverged from actual HTTP bodies in Ollama/LocalAI/MiniMax; unified model resolution preserves each existing configured/picker/routed precedence contract.
- **PC04-D05 (FIXED):** Modern Cline ignored its advertised configured model setting; its effective model and --model now agree. Legacy CLI still emits no unsupported model control.
- **PC04-D06 (FIXED):** OpenRouter and pending MiniMax resolution could read a later model selection; snapshot originating model before asynchronous preparation. MiniMax test partly hardens newly unified resolver.
- **PC04-D07 (FIXED):** Persistent adapters restarted for ineffective thinking changes and equivalent clamped effort; comparisons now reflect effective controls. Unsupported Qwen thinking budget is omitted.
- **PC04-D08 (FIXED):** Cline claimed thinking-level control while both tested legacy and modern send paths transmitted none; capability now honestly disables that control.
- **PC04-D09 (FIXED):** Changing settings during a response could relabel its final or auth-interrupted receipt and usage with the next-turn model. Host captures the dispatched model for receipt/usage/context accounting.
- **PC04-D10 (FIXED):** Model menus lost keyboard focus and accepted malformed/duplicate rows; hub model/thinking/effort visibility misrepresented capabilities. Both surfaces now preserve focus, sanitize rows, and use scoped hidden styling plus none/custom-only capability behavior.
- **PC04-D11 (FIXED):** A superseded settings write could still relay its old payload into the paired chat/settings view. Handler clears superseded relay fields; both directions reproduced red and passed after fix.

Still required:

- Run the real per-backend/account settings sweep beyond the verified Codex and Claude subset: all supported effort/thinking controls, custom/discovered models, routed overrides, and choices with unavailable model entitlement. Controlled 16-backend wire tests do not certify live model availability.

Short native/browser/process loops do not satisfy elapsed profiles; 20 native catalog requests may use cache.
- Repeat native acceptance on Windows/Linux and minimum/current supported VS Code, with genuine restart cache transitions and measured native descendant-process cleanup. Live manager cleanup passed in 657 ms for 8 observed owned processes; native post-exit wrapper snapshot alone is not that gate.

Findings:

- **PC05-D01 (FIXED):** Stream closure and terminal/cleanup exceptions could lose or duplicate responses, leave tools running, or mark a replacement turn idle. Interrupted snapshots, exactly-once terminal persistence and generation checks now pass157 host checks.
- **PC05-D02 (FIXED):** Path-only Revert used whole-file Git checkout/clean, risking unrelated developer changes. It now reverses one uniquely identified completed Edit replacement; unsupported/ambiguous/stale results refuse safely.
- **PC05-D03 (FIXED):** The latent Apply handler could report success when the editor rejected an edit or accept unsafe/stale targets. Workspace and document ownership checks plus truthful editor results now guard it. No public Apply producer was found.
- **PC05-D04 (FIXED):** Edit source location searched removed text, lost tool identity and used unsafe filename selectors. Requests now search inserted text, carry tool IDs, and update/open only the matching card.
- **PC05-D05 (FIXED):** Browser rendering/copy controls lost code and final segments/tool states, misclassified explicit fences, and exposed inaccessible/misleading diff actions. Real Chromium checks now pass; malformed/stale/detached Mermaid cleanup is verified.
- **PC05-D06 (FIXED):** Raw model HTML could imitate trusted message actions. Sanitization now retains only presentation metadata; trusted code-copy controls are inserted after sanitization.
- **PC05-D07 (FIXED):** User-message HTML-like placeholders such as <value> disappeared from the displayed prompt. A private user Markdown parser now preserves literal HTML tokens while retaining Markdown/code/link semantics and sanitization.

Still required:

- Complete prescribed S (45–60actualminutes/25meaningfuloperations), W (8actualhours/250turns), and E (24actualhours/750operations/20faults) profiles with resource/interaction gates; accelerated Chromium counts do not satisfy elapsed profiles.
- Execute full large-transcript native editor and cross-platform/resource-pressure scenarios; current native evidence is two macOS Claude turns with real Codex/Claude handoff separately.
- Public Apply is NOT_REACHABLE: no UI producer found. Host handler faults were verified, but no public suggested-edit Apply journey is claimed.
- Validate the2026-10-22 literal user HTML fix in a packaged editor journey. Local VSIX113d7142 and44targetedtests are verified; accepted full-suite REL33 remains historical. Prescribed endurance profiles remain DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **PC06-D01 (FIXED):** Second opinions rebuilt questions from rendered text and prepended an agent tag, losing Markdown and risking old-agent fanout. Original attachments were omitted. New source-ID/one-respondent dispatch preserves original source/context/files and rejects missing originals.
- **PC06-D02 (FIXED):** Unsent drafts/files followed the webview into unrelated chats; an open second-opinion menu could send an old question there. Drafts/files are now scoped to conversations for the webview lifetime and stale menus close on navigation.
- **PC06-D03 (FIXED):** IME Enter/Tab/229 and held Enter could submit or alter composing text; attachment-only busy submissions were dropped. Keyboard guards and attachment-aware queueing now pass Chromium tests.
- **PC06-D04 (FIXED):** Queue draining removed a message before dictation caused send to return. Drain now accepts before removal and preserves the independent voice draft.
- **PC06-D05 (FIXED):** Typed/menu slash commands dropped attachments, lacked a busy latch and correlated completion, or applied delayed results to another chat. Host ownership, acknowledgements, truthful errors and UI queue handling now pass.
- **PC06-D06 (FIXED):** A composer follow-up could cancel the timer holding already-drained channel amendments. Channel messages remain queued until actual dispatch; 50 controlled race bursts pass.
- **PC06-D07 (FIXED):** File picker completion or subsequent reads could attach a file to a replacement chat or newer submitted draft. Panel/conversation/generation ownership now fences delivery.
- **PC06-D08 (FIXED):** Answers without usage/session receipts lost the Second opinion affordance, including restored answers. Owned original questions now enable the action without fabricated usage.
- **PC06-D09 (FIXED):** Second opinions no longer reject available empty attachments or replace saved empty bytes with a different live payload. Saved data has precedence; missing payload data is not inferred from a zero size field.
- **PC06-D10 (FIXED):** The composer cleared submitted text and attachments before the host recorded the question; cancellation during context/settings/compaction preparation left no visible recovery route. Correlated host receipts now retain unrecorded submissions for explicit restoration in their original conversation, without overwriting newer drafts or retrying automatically. Recovery clicks are contained so slash-menu dismissal cannot erase a restored session command.

Still required:

- Complete prescribed S (45–60 actual minutes/25 meaningful operations), W (8 elapsed hours/250 turns including100drafts/50queuebursts/30opinions), E (24 elapsed hours/750operations/20faults), and P (five actual returning-user days) with resource/interaction gates.
- Run the complete F2 full-stack fixture, actual OS input-method/IME and cross-platform boundaries. Current native fixture is a small inline retry/cache task with two owned text files.
- Measure direct auxiliary title/classification/warm-worker request/spend and process lifetime. Selected conversational wrapper receipts and editor host exit pass; a full pre-exit editor ancestry snapshot was not captured.
- Verify explicit reload/shutdown draft boundaries across actual returning sessions. In-memory draft and queue behavior does not promise durable unsent content after webview disposal.
- Current package 0b542da0 passed native text recovery through LocalAI loopback and file attachment recovery through synthetic Claude CLI. Actual provider compatibility, additional adapters, close/reload boundaries, session/brainstorm paths and broader faults remain pending; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

### PC-07 — Stop, clear session, shutdown and next-turn recovery

**Status: PARTIAL.** Nov 3 bounded native validation of unchanged package 0b542da0: four LocalAI loopback cases cover changed/deleted file refresh, original message snapshots, editor-tab context isolation and cancelled-text recovery. Three additional native cases cover real file picking and attachment recovery through the packaged Claude adapter with a synthetic CLI: selected nonempty/empty bytes survive source modification/deletion; Stop during Mysti manual compaction preserves the submission alongside newer drafts and attachments; navigation retains recovery in the original conversation. Four attachment snapshots were cleaned and all seven fixture processes exited. Existing 716 targeted/compatibility checks remain prior evidence. Actual installed provider/model acceptance, image/clipboard/platform matrix, other lifecycle faults and prescribed profiles remain incomplete. No ordinary-profile installation; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Plan: [detailed case](47-feature-tests/providers-and-conversations.md).

Findings:

- **PC07-D01 (FIXED):** Lifecycle scans and shutdown completion could mutate replacement work or delete synchronously re-created sessions; activity revisions and owner checks fence every await/reentrant callback.
- **PC07-D02 (FIXED):** Base CLI wrapper descendants could keep working after Stop, including when the launcher had already exited. Both POSIX spawn paths now own process groups; captured-handle teardown reaches descendants independently of launcher liveness.
- **PC07-D03 (FIXED):** Cancelled approval controls remained actionable and repeated clicks posted duplicate decisions; scoped dismissal now disables controls and submits once.
- **PC07-D04 (FIXED):** Children detected/busy shutdown/new session labels could show obsolete Idle/zero-process state; browser state now reflects observed lifecycle.
- **PC07-D05 (FIXED):** Explicit Stop lost partial assistant history and retained busy/canvas state if the cancelled stream never returned; host snapshots useful output and settles state immediately, browser uses saved Copy identity.
- **PC07-D06 (FIXED):** Typed/menu /clear created a global conversation without updating its originating panel; New Chat after delayed dictation cancellation could erase replacement work. Both now use a host-owned reset fenced by captured panel/conversation/send generation.
- **PC07-D07 (FIXED):** Persistent CLI processes were not registered with ProviderManager and early lifecycle PIDs were lost; provider-aware tracking now registers before prompt/approval waits. Captured-process cleanup clears stale PIDs without clearing replacement ownership.
- **PC07-D08 (FIXED):** A stopped multi-agent session could emit late completion/error and mark its replacement idle; a session waiting for compaction could overwrite replacement work. Captured panel/conversation/generation and channel ownership now fence preparation, gates, stream events, terminal persistence and cleanup.
- **PC07-D09 (FIXED):** One protected shutdown click posted duplicate shutdown-blocked notices because manager and host both emitted. The lifecycle manager is now the sole publisher for both busy and child-blocked shutdowns; host only sends the success acknowledgement.
- **PC07-D10 (FIXED):** Codex could acknowledge thread/turn creation before persisting input; Stop before commit then resumed without local history, losing cross-agent context. Codex now treats prepared/interrupted turns as uncertain, replays saved transcript as context on recovery, and resumes suppression only after a matching successful completion. Covers first, later and pre-format stopped requests; Actual native wire, exact nonce response and test-owned nativejournal now verify recovery.
- **PC07-D11 (FIXED):** Actual catalog ordering caused /clear Enter/Tab to choose Clear context; changing query could execute an older selected action. Exact slash labels now get default selection, explicit arrow choices retain command ID across refresh, query changes discard old rows, and late catalogs cannot reopen closed menus.
- **PC07-D12 (FIXED):** Background title/classification managers left active jobs unsettled, omitted title workers from cancellation, could respawn after disposal, and let stale callbacks alter replacement ownership. Captured process ownership, disposed guards and extension/host disposal wiring now pass controlled tests, actual Node process-group checks and the final native exit audit. Earlier unclassified native identities are not retroactively attributed to this defect.
- **PC07-D13 (FIXED):** A rejected file reference no longer releases the Stop-held queue. The invalid explicit send preserves the draft and hold; late completion cannot dispatch retained work. An accepted explicit send still releases the hold. Qualified using an already-invalid quoted reference before the new unknown-path behavior.
- **PC07-D14 (FIXED):** The composer cleared submitted text and attachments before the host recorded the question; cancellation during context/settings/compaction preparation left no visible recovery route. Correlated host receipts now retain unrecorded submissions for explicit restoration in their original conversation, without overwriting newer drafts or retrying automatically. Recovery clicks are contained so slash-menu dismissal cannot erase a restored session command.

Still required:

- Complete S (45–60 actual minutes/25 meaningful operations plus restart), W (8 hours/250 turns and 50 Stop/recovery cycles per backend), and E (24 hours/750 operations, 100 panel/exit races and resource gates). Debugging across fresh profiles does not satisfy these elapsed-time profiles.
- Run the live near-final Stop race, shared returning-user continuity profile, remaining 14 backend adapters/transports, Windows native/.cmd, Linux and minimum supported VS Code. Current native evidence is macOS arm64/VS Code 1.141.0 with Claude persistent CLI and Codex app-server.
- Measure sustained RSS/CPU/webview memory/listeners/connections and latency. Direct constructor-cached auxiliary inference count/spend remains unmeasured; observed process-tree cleanup does not count those requests or catch every child between 100ms samples.
- Default protectActiveChildren conservatively blocks resident runtime children behind wrappers. The test separately verified that default and successful public shutdown with protection disabled only in its disposable profile; runtime-versus-tool child classification remains unsupported.
- Use the full F2 developer fixture. This native run used small inline tasks and owned canaries. New-chat isolation verifies absence of the previous conversation nonce; workspace project memory is intentionally shared.
- Current package 0b542da0 passed native text recovery through LocalAI loopback and file attachment recovery through synthetic Claude CLI. Actual provider compatibility, additional adapters, close/reload boundaries, session/brainstorm paths and broader faults remain pending; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **PC08-D01 (FIXED):** Delayed history switches could overwrite newer navigation, a new send or replacement panel. Panel/conversation/generation plus navigation revisions now fence completion, including reselecting the current row.
- **PC08-D02 (FIXED):** Deleting a conversation reset only its requesting panel and could create two empty replacements for the last chat. All viewing panels now stop owned work and receive valid replacements; the manager-created last-chat replacement is reused.
- **PC08-D03 (FIXED):** Public New Conversation created global state and broadcast no transcript payload. It now routes through the owned host reset and prefers the active editor chat over the last sender; with no panel it opens a chat.
- **PC08-D04 (FIXED):** Cancelled Mysti coordinator tails retained generation ownership after navigation. Reset now revokes generation before cancellation callbacks, preventing late persistence and terminal events into the replacement view.
- **PC08-D05 (FIXED):** Explicit missing/deleted conversation IDs fell back to the current chat; mismatched stored map keys and IDs could target another record. Missing explicit targets now fail and stored IDs are repaired with original recovery data retained.
- **PC08-D06 (FIXED):** Malformed stored message/metadata structures could break replay, and partially repaired timelines could hide intact canonical text. Structural repairs and whole-timeline fallback keep readable content and park the untouched original.
- **PC08-D07 (FIXED):** Switching the manager current conversation was not persisted. Selection now saves and survives a serialized reload.
- **PC08-D08 (FIXED):** Repeated failed history writes produced duplicate error alerts. One alert per outage resets after a successful save.
- **PC08-D09 (FIXED):** History rows could not open from the keyboard; focused Delete was invisible and late title refresh lost focus. Native buttons, visible focus, Escape and ID-bound focus restoration now pass Chromium tests.
- **PC08-D10 (FIXED):** Non-string arbitrary tool paths, commands and Task prompts could crash history summary formatting. Display guards omit invalid summaries while preserving exact stored tool input details.
- **PC08-D11 (FIXED):** Deleting a conversation left its background jobs and coordinator-owned approval gates active. Deletion now cancels only locally executing jobs for that conversation; late cancelled/model-metadata completion cannot persist or recreate deleted history. Unrelated and other-window job records remain untouched.

Still required:

- Complete required S/W/E/P shared profiles, including P five actual days/1,000turns/100conversations/fiverestarts and E24hours/750operations/large nestedstore workload. Debugging and freshprofiles are not a soak.
- Run native store-corruption/partial-write/disk-full/storage-rejection/future-schema and realupgrade recovery with originalbytes preserved. Current evidence uses controlled managers/serializedsnapshots and ownedtemporaryEISDIR, not an actual VSCode disk-full run.
- Validate successful generated titles through the product UI and characterize titleworker latency/earlynonzeroexits before tuningthe5sdeadline. All21 finalnative titles used honestfallback; one directcoldprobe succeededafter12.15s.
- Cover remaining backend/platform/minimumeditor matrix, full F2 developerfixture, sustainedresources/listeners/processgrowth and accurateproviderusage/spend. Nativeevidence is macOSarm64/VSCode1.141.0/Claude2.1.288+Codex0.162.0 with inlinecodingtasks.

**Status: PARTIAL.** Eight isolation defect groups fixed. Final full regression passed 14,655 tests across 502 files; release gates passed. Bounded native acceptance passed with actual Codex/Claude conversations across two ordinary VS Code windows and distinct profiles: Stop, sibling completion, inactive-tab close, context ownership and recovery verified; all 405 observed process identities exited. Independent artifact/process/10,000-file audit passed. Across four preserved native attempts: 13 conversational inputs and 19 auxiliary input writes. Required elapsed profiles and broader combinations remain unrun.

Findings:

- **PC09-D01 (FIXED):** Persistent CLI reuse ignored its original working directory, so later turns after first-root changes ran in the old workspace; an empty folder array also failed startup. Spawn cwd is now part of reuse compatibility and empty folders use the existing host-cwd fallback.
- **PC09-D02 (FIXED):** Removed or already answered ordinary question controls could still post stale/duplicate answers. Connected-container and settled-state guards reject them.
- **PC09-D03 (FIXED):** Ordinary question data and timers collided on reused native IDs; cross-panel/stale/duplicate/malformed answers and queued old timers could affect a replacement question. Unique host delivery IDs, owner/conversation/turn checks, one-time consumption and scoped lifecycle cleanup now guard UI, channel and timeout paths. Native tool results retain the original tool ID.
- **PC09-D04 (FIXED):** Explicitly targeted public context/settings/provider/history notifications were widened into broadcasts. An explicit panel ID now always wins.
- **PC09-D05 (FIXED):** Editor Add/Clear Context commands mutated the hidden default bucket and notified all chats. They now resolve one chat owner and update that panel bucket only.
- **PC09-D06 (FIXED):** Delayed file reads could persist old context after clear/close/navigation and concurrent reads could add duplicates. Captured owner/bucket checks and post-read deduplication reject these writes.
- **PC09-D07 (FIXED):** Detached tabs opened in the same millisecond shared IDs, overwrote state and could dispose a sibling. UUID panel IDs preserve independent ownership.
- **PC09-D08 (FIXED):** Question continuations rebuilt mode/access/effort from shared defaults, potentially broadening a read-only turn after another chat changed settings. Continuations retain their own prior policy, including after a provider selection change. Final full regression passed.

Still required:

- Execute required elapsed S/W/E/P profiles; accelerated fixtures and bounded native scenarios do not substitute for them.
- Exercise same-profile global-store concurrency, broader workspace combinations, other providers/platforms and minimum-editor version.
- Native repeated-ID, permission and ordinary-question collisions remain controlled-fixture coverage only.

### PC-10 — Conversation forks and branch history

**Status: PARTIAL.** Thirteen defect groups fixed. Final regression passed 14,726 tests across 506 files; typecheck, lint and release gates passed. Bounded native acceptance passed on the first attempt: five real Claude/Codex requests, actual Read/tool segment, independent branch/original continuations, Cancel, checkpoint restore and Undo. Root disk/file/process and complete five-reply semantic audits passed; all 163 observed process identities exited. Full F2, assistant-message6 fork reachability and required elapsed P coverage remain incomplete.

Plan: [detailed case](47-feature-tests/providers-and-conversations.md).

Findings:

- **PC10-D01 (FIXED):** Forked agent config, thinking, nested tool inputs/diffs and extensible metadata aliased the original. Normalized complete JSON values are now detached.
- **PC10-D02 (FIXED):** Fork-and-rewind created/activated a branch before confirmation or disk success, including cancel, missing/unavailable checkpoint and engine failure. It now validates and restores first, then activates the branch.
- **PC10-D03 (FIXED):** Rewind accepted foreign/mismatched anchors, duplicate actions and stale confirmation/Undo after navigation, new turn, close or replacement. Captured conversation/message/generation/navigation/workspace ownership and one transaction identity guard these paths.
- **PC10-D04 (FIXED):** Late checkpoint completions enabled the same message ID in a different fork or replacement panel. Original store records are updated only while present, and UI events include/capture their actual conversation owner.
- **PC10-D05 (FIXED):** Fork cleared native work but retained old context usage/session lifecycle indicator. Fork now resets usage/lifecycle and publishes sessionCleared.
- **PC10-D06 (FIXED):** Rewind omitted active response termination from the UI and could restore shared files while another panel worked. It snapshots/stops the current visible response and refuses while another chat is active.
- **PC10-D07 (FIXED):** Late rewind/Undo completion after navigation hid actual restored files or actionable rollback failure. Global notices now report disk effects/recovery failures without writing to the new chat.
- **PC10-D08 (FIXED):** Rewind continued without its promised safety snapshot and lacked recovery after destructive failure. Safety is now mandatory; failed reset attempts rollback, with an explicit recovery checkpoint if rollback fails.
- **PC10-D09 (FIXED):** Blanket git clean deleted files excluded from safety after ignore rules changed; reset itself could overwrite unbacked ignored obstacles. Blanket clean is removed; pinned path-only preflight refuses unbacked target conflicts without copying ignored content.
- **PC10-D10 (FIXED):** Queued rewind ignored owner cancellation and could retarget work after folder changes, including A→B→A. Owner/root/revision checks run before reset; once reset starts, it completes or recovers against captured paths.
- **PC10-D11 (FIXED):** Open fork menus had stale checkpoint availability and missing explicit conversation identity. Availability refresh, owner validation and captured conversationId payloads now match host ownership.
- **PC10-D12 (FIXED):** Fork controls were invisible to keyboard users, dismissal/focus could race, and held Enter could accidentally activate an action. Focus visibility, menu keyboard navigation, immediate dismissal listeners and repeat-key guards fix those interactions.
- **PC10-D13 (FIXED):** Fork menus could extend beyond the bottom edge or remain offscreen after resize. Viewport bounds and resize handling keep the reachable menu visible.

Still required:

- Execute the prescribed elapsed five-day P profile; accelerated 50 forks/depth10/20-deletion checks do not replace elapsed coverage.
- Run the full planned F2 issue-tracker fixture and remaining native failure/race/provider/platform combinations. Current native fixture has five files.
- The public fork menu is available on user messages only; planned assistant message6 action is unreachable. Native acceptance used user message5; controlled manager tests include message6.
- Native restart of branched history remains unrun; actual disk persistence after normal exit was verified. Full checkpoint semantics and external writes concurrent with Git restore remain AR coverage.

### PC-11 — Export, import, share links and clipboard

**Status: PARTIAL.** Bounded export/import/share/clipboard scenarios pass after thirteen defect groups were fixed. Seven real Claude/Codex conversational inputs, actual file dialogs/clipboard/URI handling, concurrent import recovery and independent semantic/disk/process audits pass on package 69c846d2d0f6. The final acknowledgement-only revision passes 14,834 tests and a separate fresh-profile native smoke on package e329e670b064. Native mid-token import, full F2 breadth and elapsed W/P profiles remain unrun.

Plan: [detailed case](47-feature-tests/providers-and-conversations.md).

Findings:

- **PC11-D01 (FIXED):** File export had no shipped UI producer and clipboard export labeling implied a saved file. Public JSON/Markdown file actions, immediate Import, and truthful Copy labels now exist.
- **PC11-D02 (FIXED):** Clipboard controls claimed success before IO completed, failed to support reliable retries, and consumed stale acknowledgements. Scoped per-request results and connected origin checks now govern feedback.
- **PC11-D03 (FIXED):** File imports could replace a newer chat after a delayed dialog/read, create orphan conversations, or run twice for a duplicate request. Panel, conversation, navigation, generation and request guards prevent stale transitions.
- **PC11-D04 (FIXED):** Successful file import retained the old provider session and active stream. It now preserves the visible partial response, cancels only its owning panel, clears native history and rejects old stream tails.
- **PC11-D05 (FIXED):** Copy/file/share routes leaked IO exceptions or reported the wrong destination. Host error/cancel results now identify the requested action, and clipboard writes execute in order.
- **PC11-D06 (FIXED):** Highly compressible conversations could produce short URLs only because share export silently truncated text/history. Exact limit metadata now triggers full JSON fallback before loss and short links disclose omitted structured metadata.
- **PC11-D07 (FIXED):** JSON import discarded supported attribution and structured history. Supported fields are now retained with fresh message IDs and independent storage.
- **PC11-D08 (FIXED):** Markdown was advertised but lacked a parser; malformed JSONL could import a silent partial transcript. Framed Mysti Markdown supports exact text/roles, legacy Markdown is explicitly best-effort, and malformed JSONL fails atomically.
- **PC11-D09 (FIXED):** Imported tool/checkpoint metadata could confer local restore authority. Imported checkpoints are stripped, tools are historical only, and both UI and host reject historical Revert before file access.
- **PC11-D10 (FIXED):** External URI import broadcast an empty conversation change to existing panels. It now opens the stored import in its own tab without clearing active siblings.
- **PC11-D11 (FIXED):** Unvalidated imported tool.status entered innerHTML and injected image markup. Manager normalization and independent renderer escaping/enum-only classes prevent injection while retaining historical Input/Output.
- **PC11-D12 (FIXED):** Import format limitations disappeared during navigation and external URI imports omitted repair diagnostics. Destination-owned notices and URI diagnostics now report the actual retained format and limitations.
- **PC11-D13 (FIXED):** The live JSON import exposed an incorrect format:markdown acknowledgement inherited from the Copy default. Import now omits the optional export-format field; the actual parser diagnostic still describes any format limitations.

Still required:

- Native active import occurred before first assistant output; actual mid-token native import remains unrun (controlled host partial-tail cases pass).
- Required elapsed W and P profiles remain unrun; accelerated 100 JSON transfers/25 share opens do not substitute for elapsed duration.
- Broader external format/provider/platform compatibility and full F2 repository coverage remain bounded by the available fixtures.
- OS-default URI application routing and actual OS clipboard denial are not covered by the successful owned-editor URI and clipboard tests; controlled failure checks pass.

Findings:

- **PC12-D01 (FIXED):** Copilot Auto exposes effort controls although its adapter omits effort for Auto.
- **PC12-D02 (FIXED):** Malformed/unknown/older-schema capability state can crash or offer unsupported controls.
- **PC12-D03 (FIXED):** Tool proposals and missing results claim completion and can acquire file-effect or Revert authority.
- **PC12-D04 (FIXED):** Host plan cards ignore declared planMode:none or treat non-native events as native plans.
- **PC12-D05 (FIXED):** Notification-only tool transports dispatch unrestricted tools under Ask and cannot guarantee pre-execution consent.
- **PC12-D06 (FIXED):** Cursor Read Only omitted its installed CLI documented --mode plan flag; it now requests that explicit mode instead of treating absent --force as read-only enforcement.
- **PC12-D07 (FIXED):** Shared collaborator/delegate and legacy subagent renderers still converted proposed or unreported tools into execution/success checkmarks after the foreground renderer was corrected.
- **PC12-D08 (FIXED):** Real Copilot Auto-to-explicit continuation passes a fabricated local session ID to native --resume, causing session-not-found. Adapter declares prompt-history continuity and already replays conversation; correction must honor that contract.
- **PC12-D09 (FIXED):** Switching back to Claude resumes its native session while suppressing intervening peer turns; Claude cannot review Copilot’s patch despite a correct independent diagnosis. Automated truth-table markers did not detect the missing handoff; root semantic review did.
- **PC12-D10 (FIXED):** Claude native result.is_error is treated as ordinary text, so an unsuccessful turn can appear completed and falsely acknowledge history delivery. Failure-result parser and public-send regression are being corrected.

Still required:

- Successful explicit Copilot model inference remains unverified: mai-code-1.1-flash and gpt-5.4 were rejected when requested explicitly; no claim that every explicit model is unavailable.
- Real inference/capability and authority matrix across remaining13 adapters, service/account/transport transitions and actual external-tool policy boundaries remain unrun. Fresh readiness/menu checks do not establish authentication or inference.
- Full F2 fixture, ordinary native restart/upgrade-schema and broader platform/account coverage remain unrun.
- Required elapsed W/P profiles remain unrun; accelerated controls and time between manual continuations do not satisfy them.

### PB-01 — Claude Code

**Status: PARTIAL.** Bounded evidence is partial: the first real run completed 19 replies, approved and denied native tools, invalid-model recovery, two chat tabs and four independent code oracles. Seven product defects were fixed and the final source suite passed 15,028 tests across 517 files. The fresh run with corrected harness and pending-card messaging was blocked by Claude session quota on its first request. Full 25-turn acceptance, native question/plan submission, Stop, attachments, compaction, F2 and elapsed profiles remain incomplete.

Plan: [detailed case](47-feature-tests/providers-and-conversations.md).

Findings:

- **PB01-D01 (FIXED):** Duplicate, dismissed, replaced or foreign/stale plan callbacks can change mode and start work; delayed timers can rebind replacement plan cards. Nine controlled browser/host failures reproduced.
- **PB01-D02 (FIXED):** Claude parser keeps hasStreamedText across turns, dropping a later valid final-only result, including native command output.
- **PB01-D03 (FIXED):** Parallel Claude tool-result batches in one stdout buffer are reordered around text and later result batches.
- **PB01-D04 (FIXED):** Malformed initialization response can leave the request waiting, while a late duplicate initialization error after successful setup can terminate a later turn.
- **PB01-D05 (FIXED):** Malformed AskUserQuestion payloads can crash rendering or create invisible pending questions/timers. Twelve provider and six UI/host controlled failures reproduced; shared schema validation and visible error/recovery now pass focused checks. Packaged native acceptance pending.
- **PB01-D06 (FIXED):** Native /compact ignored error/auth_error chunks, announced completion and changed measured usage despite failure. Four controlled failures reproduced; the host now reports the failure and preserves usage state.
- **PB01-D07 (FIXED):** The native interactive bridge described a pending question or plan as denied or cancelled. Its message now explains that Mysti is displaying a pending card and will deliver the user response as a scoped follow-up, while retaining native denial authority.

Still required:

- After Claude quota resets, complete the corrected 25-reply packaged native workflow and independently audit actual replies, persistence, code and control receipts.
- Complete actual question/plan submission, Stop with queued work, attachments, native compaction and policy-enforced attempted-write denial; safe model refusal alone is partial evidence.
- Execute F2 and native restart/upgrade, model/effort and context-pressure scenarios; complete 30 real resume/interrupt cycles.
- Run required elapsed W/E/P profiles; accelerated source checks and the 19-reply run do not satisfy them.

### PB-02 — OpenAI Codex

**Status: PARTIAL.** F03 archive/restore now has separate local qualification with 63 API checks, 14 browser groups, 52 focused controls, named migration rollback/reapply and 11 retained regression invocations. F01/F02/F04/F05/F06 and 12 bug cases remain passing on the final local workload. Native32 still ends after 17 completed tasks with provider quota failure at 18; no task 19 or final native acceptance. Native28 remains the accepted native checkpoint. Main Mysti provider-mode recovery was separately recorded as PC12-A43; its tested VSIX is not installed. Oct20 fresh Codex functional read now passes on REL28 with gpt-6.1-sol/low: one main input, one title, exact file output and durable reply, no retry. The first warm-process wrapper failure is preserved; R5 permits a fully closed empty warm replacement without increasing the one-turn budget. This bounded check does not establish full provider acceptance or N32 recovery.

Plan: [detailed case](47-feature-tests/providers-and-conversations.md).

Findings:

- **PB02-D01 (FIXED):** Stale or unknown RPC errors could kill a healthy persistent process serving a later request. Transport failure now requires ownership by an active request.
- **PB02-D02 (FIXED):** Interrupted-turn recovery used a legacy history replay path that bypassed acknowledgement tracking and could lose older peer instructions. Codex now uses shared acknowledged-history recovery.
- **PB02-D03 (FIXED):** Delayed notifications and control requests could affect another turn. Thread/turn ownership checks and bounded pre-acknowledgement buffering now isolate text, usage, failures, questions and approvals.
- **PB02-D04 (FIXED):** Malformed initialization, configuration, thread or turn replies could leave a request waiting. Malformed replies and excessive pre-acknowledgement output now fail visibly, evict the owned transport and permit explicit recovery.
- **PB02-D05 (FIXED):** A provider-owned model selected in the UI could be replaced by the static Codex default. Provider model ownership and resolved host routing now preserve the selection; eight actual retry turns verified requested, returned and persisted gpt-6.1-sol.
- **PB02-Q01 (FIXED):** Codex-generated retryAfterMs parses asctime HTTP-date in local time rather than UTC; reproducible incorrect delay across timezones. Existing14-check oracle misses this portability boundary.
- **PB02-D06 (FIXED):** Distinct Codex assistant items were concatenated without paragraph boundaries. Item-aware buffering now preserves separators, including LF and CRLF split across deltas.
- **PB02-D07 (FIXED):** Background Claude title/classification workers inherited local tools, hooks and MCP integrations. A shared restricted text-only launch policy now isolates optional workers; native request accounting is being added to the test harness.
- **PB02-D08 (FIXED):** Idle pre-warmed helpers could expire at the Claude CLI stdin deadline. Titles, suggestions and classification now start a text-only worker only once the prompt is ready.
- **PB02-Q02 (FIXED):** Fresh generated test additions were inserted before an existing final test rather than appended, violating the explicit exact-prefix preservation requirement. Model acknowledged the error.
- **PB02-Q03 (FIXED):** Fresh retryAfterMs rejects valid zero-padded asctime day format (Sun Nov 06...) while accepting the space-padded equivalent.
- **PB02-Q04 (FIXED):** Fresh RFC850 two-digit-year mapping starts in the current century and only subtracts 100, so future dates crossing into the next century are interpreted as expired.
- **PB02-D09 (FIXED):** Valid non-ASCII Codex text is silently corrupted when process-output chunks split a UTF-8 character; each Buffer was decoded independently. Accented text, Arabic and emoji reproduce with no provider error.
- **PB02-Q05 (FIXED):** During actual attachment review, the model correctly read the 1500 ms deployment retry budget but incorrectly called it the retry cap. Source still defaults to a 1000 ms cap; the image does not configure it.
- **PB02-D10 (FIXED):** An invalid nonempty saved Codex profile silently falls back to the default profile and sends the prompt. Whitespace around an otherwise valid profile is validated after trimming but forwarded untrimmed.
- **PB02-D11 (PARTIAL):** Mysti starts a named Codex profile using --profile NAME app-server, but pinned Codex CLI0.162.0 explicitly rejects --profile for the app-server subcommand. Valid named profiles fail before initialization completes or model input is sent.
- **PB02-D12 (FIXED):** Optional title/suggestion/classification workers decoded each stdout Buffer separately, corrupting Unicode split across process chunks. Each worker now owns an incremental decoder.
- **PB02-D14 (FIXED):** Duplicate helper IDs let selecting a second plan dispatch the first plan. The classifier now gives every plan, question and option a unique local selection handle while preserving valid distinct IDs and all content.
- **PB02-D13 (FIXED):** Host initialState could arrive before chat.js registered its message listener; the 12-second fallback then allowed sends with bootstrap provider and effort instead of saved settings. Initial state now waits for the current document readiness signal and sends wait until it is applied.
- **PB02-D15 (FIXED):** Title length clamping could split an astral Unicode character into an unpaired surrogate. Fallback titles also implied omitted content when only surrounding whitespace was removed. The original length bound is retained while keeping whole code points.
- **PB02-D16 (FIXED):** Five-second title budget can expire before native CLI startup and inference complete, leaving fallback text. All3 bounded live examples showed this timing and no title output. Titles now have a20second async bound matching suggestions.
- **PB02-D17 (FIXED):** Suggestion helper results/errors were sent after await without checking whether panel, conversation or turn still owned them, allowing stale results to overwrite a new view. Captured owner and per-operation guards now suppress stale delivery.
- **PB02-Q06 (PARTIAL):** In native turn22 the main model described application persistence restart as already completed, although the supplied browser review only described its method and the persistence test began38seconds later.

Still required:

- Retain Native28 main/helper shutdown limits:8 complete main terminals and2 unknown;37 helper submissions yielded32 retained unvalidated text returns and5 unknown terminals. Passive87 kernel exits,17 unknown events and34 discovery gaps remain partial. All538 sampled native/ordinary identities are gone, four runtime ports closed and PG stopped once; process disappearance does not invent exit/stderr success. Earlier Native25 and other failed-run gaps remain in immutable reports.
- Establish supported native named-profile loading when a compatible CLI mechanism is available. Pinned CLI0.162.0 rejects profiles for app-server; retain actionable errors and require explicit base-configuration selection.
- Preserve all 32 native trees and original failures; N28 stays accepted, N32 stays quota-failed at 18 with no task 19 or finalacceptance. F03 local source/runtime/evidence are now frozen after 11 retained invocations; no native acceptance inherited. Any native continuation needs a fresh qualified attempt and exact source/history joins; never replay N32. PB03 has now completed its one bounded attempt and is separately recorded; do not replay N32.
- Initial/final all12 API/ORM, first native tests/lints/build, six-case browser and application-restart persistence, actual editor history restoration and final handoff now pass. Preserve earlier failed startup/split evidence. Retain wording limits: reply1 intro command count, reply4 application-versus-browser process-exit phrasing and task-scoped remaining-uncertainty list. Broader Q06 reliability and ordinary-mistake recovery remain unverified.
- Complete model/profile cleanup overlap, native questions/plans, native thread resume and over-context compaction/error recovery. Thirty reset cycles and controlled app-server crash/recovery are verified separately; editor restart restored saved history into a fresh native thread.
- D16 title and D17 suggestion-ownership fixes remain release-validated. Native28 helper37 submissions have32 text returns and5 unknown terminals; title/classification/suggestion semantic quality remains unvalidated by these transport counts. D17 source/test852 and VSIX remain frozen; historical Native25 helper limits remain separately retained.
- Complete elapsed S/W/E/P profiles: S45–60minutes with25 meaningful operations and two ordinary mistakes/recovery, W8hours/250turns, E24hours/750turns, P5days/1000turns. Native28 lasted36.64355minutes with18 guided feature tasks and does not satisfy S; earlier shorter/injected sessions remain insufficient.
- Continue inventory order with active PB-03. Its first native request failed upstream with UNSUPPORTED_CLIENT; detailed PB03 records own the result and remaining coverage.
- N32 helper35 inputs have28 terminal outcomes and7 unknown; semantic quality is only bounded where content was retained. Observer2 ended at maximum duration with incomplete collection. Quota error surfaced to the UI without a retry loop, but post-error composer/persistence/retry behavior was not captured before harness exit.
- One Oct20 public read-only functional request now passes with native/public/durable joins. Broader live model/profile, approval/question, resume/compaction and quota-error recovery checks remain separate; no automatic replay of N32.

Findings:

- **PB03-H01 (FIXED):** Canonical JSON value comparison accepts valid nested null-prototype JSONC objects without weakening parsed-key rejection.
- **PB03-H02 (FIXED):** Canonical owned destinations checked before writes; redirected/dangling .gemini paths and existing settings reject without external writes.
- **PB03-H03 (FIXED):** Custom GEMINI_CLI_HOME is treated as a home path and .gemini is appended, matching pinned CLI source; actual nested settings and policies are now inspected.
- **PB03-E01 (BLOCKED):** Existing Gemini Code Assist for individuals service rejected the installed CLI with UNSUPPORTED_CLIENT before generating output.
- **PB03-D01 (FIXED):** Known unsupported-client rejection is rendered with terminal warnings and repeated eligibility diagnostics instead of a concise explanation.

Still required:

- Service/client eligibility must change before a fresh qualified native attempt; preserve the failed first attempt and do not replay it. No account, billing or credential changes are authorized by this test.
- On supported service access, verify actual model usage, answer/file-read correctness, tools, public/native/durable attribution, Stop and failure recovery. The failed startup provides none of this acceptance.
- Auxiliary title terminal/result remains unknown; disappearance is not proof of success. Future instrumentation must retain terminal outcomes without changing this historical record.
- Execute all required PB-03 scenarios and elapsed S/W/E/P profiles.

Findings:

- **PB04-D01 (FIXED):** Source-identified .clinerules write/restore can overwrite concurrent-panel content or manual edits; cleanup lacks shared ownership and compare-before-restore.
- **PB04-D02 (FIXED):** Cline logs immediate raw stderr before downstream error redaction; synthetic-secret regression is needed.
- **PB04-D03 (FIXED):** Modern --model override support conflicts with modelSelection:none capability metadata.
- **PB04-D04 (FIXED):** Plan-card execution preserved read-only access and could silently remain Plan or offer unsupported approvals.
- **PB04-D05 (FIXED):** Current Cline tool/reasoning/error content envelopes were ignored.
- **PB04-D06 (FIXED):** Cline custom spawn did not own a POSIX process group; ordinary descendants survived cancellation or launcher completion.
- **PB04-D07 (FIXED):** Cline advertised enhancement but invoked obsolete --print/--output-format options; installed modernrootCLI has no qualified tool-free text route.
- **PB04-D08 (FIXED):** Modern Cline aggregate tokens were displayed as current context; native cache fields were lost and vendor-based normalization misread inclusive inputs.
- **PB04-D09 (FIXED):** ProviderManager.cancelRequest immediately SIGKILLs the tracked process after delegating graceful cancellation, bypassing provider grace and native abort acknowledgment. It also looks up the handle after reentrant cancellation callbacks.
- **PB04-D10 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Cline can run a detached global npm update before parsing --version; Mysti-owned discovery probes and requests lacked the supported startup-update guard.
- **PB04-D11 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Source review identifies a cancellation race: the native global handler exits with code 1 on a second SIGTERM, while Cline request finalization can send a duplicate TERM during graceful cancellation. N6 A acknowledged abort before its second signal; B had a second signal after 11 ms and remains UNKNOWN. This timing does not establish B service cancellation.
- **PB04-D12 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Inherited inactivity and process-timeout cleanup could send TERM again through Cline ask/finally, cutting short native abort acknowledgment. Both real-child timeout routes reproduced the race.
- **PB04-D13 (FIXED_LOCAL):** A process killed by a signal after partial output could appear successful because its exit code is null; an already-fired close event could also leave the request waiting until timeout.
- **PB04-D14 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Cline final done/run_result error text is replaced by generic Cline stopped: error; actual invalid-model rejection loses actionable model diagnostic.
- **PB04-D15 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Modern Cline Plan/read-only omits explicit auto-approval policy. Installed3.0.61 defaults wildcard autoApprove=true or inherits a persisted value; its Plan command filter permits a Python file-write command despite public read-only authority.
- **PB04-D16 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Installed Cline shared-hub backend rejects tool approval when session interactive=false before publishing approval.requested, bypassing local desktop callback. Actual read_files unavailable under corrected explicitfalse policy.
- **PB04-D17 (FIXED_LOCAL):** A responseComplete arriving after local Stop but before requestCancelled drained queued work because Stop only posted an asynchronous host message.

Still required:

- Complete honest modern Cline unsupported Plan/read-only preflight/capability correction and Stop queue race release. Native read-only support remains unavailable; no unchanged retry. Then distinct bounded native Stop/queued-writer suppression with explicit supported full-access test authority and fresh same-panel read.
- Continue D12 native timeout acceptance and remaining mandatory backend, capability/continuity and returning-user gates. N9 proves four actual startup-guard entries, not complete updater behavior or the missing probe outcome. N8 closes bounded rules Save/healthy-sibling completion only; preserve its third auxiliary terminal UNKNOWN, N6 failure/B UNKNOWN, N5 fourth-probe UNKNOWN and unused old REL7 N7 preparation.
- DEFERRED_BY_USER — Complete S: 45–60 actual minutes and at least 25 real meaningful turns, two ordinary mistakes/recoveries and one restart, with full F1 then F2 and all mandatory backend gates. Do not dispatch this endurance campaign while the user preference remains in force.
- DEFERRED_BY_USER — Complete W: eight elapsed hours and 250 real turns across ten tasks, 25 cancellations, 20 panel/context switches, ten idle/restart cycles, five changed requirements, prescribed task mix, lunch and sleep/wake. Do not dispatch this endurance campaign while the user preference remains in force.
- DEFERRED_BY_USER — Complete single-shot E: 24 elapsed hours and 750 turns/operations, hourly idle windows and at least 20 fault/recovery events, with the required 60-second resource/latency sampling. W250 is not E750; only campaigns meeting every requirement can share credit. Do not dispatch this endurance campaign while the user preference remains in force.
- Preserve original N9 strict FAIL and missing-probe UNKNOWN, earlier N5/N6/N8 unknowns, the local/direct-probe checker failures and old unlaunched N7. Local deadline comparison and fresh direct readiness do not attribute N9 cause/sender or justify a product timeout increase. D08 completed-turn scope is bounded; interrupted delivery, per-turn durable cache/cost/input and other listed branches remain outside acceptance. No N9 native replay is needed.
- User explicitly chose to continue functional checks and defer endurance on2026-10-15. Long S/W/E campaigns remain unexecuted and deferred; their requirements are not waived or counted as passed. Continue bounded functional testing and fixes.

### PB-05 — GitHub Copilot

**Status: PARTIAL.** Nov 3 current Copilot 1.0.83 Auto functional checks pass through the exact packaged native VS Code UI: actual read-only view, scoped file repair with an independent oracle, prompt-history continuation and new-panel isolation. Three requests selected gpt-6-luna; staged and unstaged developer notes preserved, fixture tests pass, all 114 observed native/provider/discovery PIDs gone. Then fixed current JSONC/camelCase login readiness and malformed-config false positives: 9 red reproductions, 16 focused cases, 144 related tests green, updated VSIX 85d38cd7 built and validated. Login-only update is locally verified and packaged, not ordinary-profile installed. Original N07/N08 strict failures and unknown historical N08 cause remain unchanged. Full PB-05 acceptance stays IN_PROGRESS; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER. Further Nov 3 native checks verify exact explicit-model/Low routing, clear unavailable-model errors, draft preservation and exact Auto recovery on new package 85d38cd7. gpt-5.4 is service-unavailable, so no explicit-inference acceptance. Native harness acknowledgement/answer assertions corrected; no product model-selection defect established. Further Nov 3 found and fixed the production JSON5 import failure that prevented Copilot collaboration, plus automatic retries of permanent model rejections. Current VSIX d382a983 passes a real Claude/Codex/Copilot review and exact transcript restoration after an ordinary editor restart in an owned test profile. Disk storage is verified before requests; three real requests, zero during restart, unchanged fixture/staged diff. 197 related tests, 59 final retry regressions and three browser checks pass. Original test-harness failures remain retained; no ordinary-profile installation. Nov 3 bounded permission/Stop follow-up: shared prompt instructions incorrectly offered edit approval under Read Only. Fixed effective-policy precedence in both prompt builders, with 16 red cases becoming green and 396 related tests passing. New VSIX 0c113be7 passes real Copilot file-read/no-edit guidance and Stop/recovery with a deliberately frozen native child, launcher exiting first, replacement overlap and preserved newer draft. Files/staged changes stay intact; no native write tool was attempted. Original failed wording run retained; no ordinary-profile install or endurance run.

Findings:

- **PB05-D01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Modern root session.error events on JSON stdout were dropped, producing a generic failure or no-response message when stderr was empty.
- **PB05-D02 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Raw stderr chunks could expose issuing-process credentials, including split fragments; supplemental authentication handling could repeat an already-reported raw diagnostic.
- **PB05-D03 (FIXED_LOCAL):** The asynchronous spawn-error callback logged the raw Node Error object, whose spawnargs can disclose the full prompt and credentials on ENOENT.
- **PB05-D04 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Installed Copilot can emit assistant.message without deltas under its existing stream:false setting, but Mysti unconditionally ignores the complete-message event. Valid reply text is lost.
- **PB05-D05 (FIXED_LOCAL):** An already-prefixed native diagnostic rendered as Error: Error: ... in the chat.
- **PB05-D06 (FIXED_LOCAL):** An effective unlisted custom model showed only Custom… in the composer and actions label.
- **PB05-D07 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Modern failed tool completion with error.message and no result.content produced literal empty quotes instead of its diagnostic.
- **PB05-H01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Frozen permission oracle assumes object edit arguments; actual Copilot1.0.94 uses supported apply_patch string input and detailed write-rule denial text.
- **PB05-H02 (PARTIAL):** N08 one --version probe retained invocation-start and empty output but no terminal, preventing strict acceptance despite verified main Stop/recovery behavior.
- **PB05-D08 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Current JSONC/camelCase login fields were unsupported. Strict camelCase JSON could report a signed-in account as unauthenticated; comments, unreadable config and malformed legacy identity arrays could falsely report authentication.
- **PB05-H03 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Native test waited on the picker model instead of the honored customModel field, then cumulative counters cascaded; broad event-text matching could count expected tokens in the user message as an assistant answer. No product model-selection defect was established.
- **PB05-D09 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Source tests accepted login but webpack resolved JSON5 ESM default-only exports; the named parser call failed and collaboration skipped authenticated Copilot.
- **PB05-D10 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** A real Codex unavailable-model rejection triggered a repeat collaboration request; the bounded wrapper stopped the extra paid request.
- **PB05-H04 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Harness assumed startup provider selection, counted intermediate errors as independent terminals, read collapsed card headers, and attempted history restart in VS Code in-memory test storage.
- **PB05-D11 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Read Only blocked edits but the shared prompt still told the model to ask for edit approval or edit automatically, yielding an unusable approval offer.

Still required:

- Bounded Copilot read/write/isolation, explicit-model error-to-Auto recovery, three-provider review, disk-backed transcript restoration, Read Only guidance/canary preservation and frozen-child Stop/replacement checks now have evidence, each tied to its package. Remaining mandatory BP capability/fault/context-budget/F2 work is not fully accepted. Successful explicit Copilot model/Low-effort inference remains unavailable; do not repeat unchanged rejected models. Preserve original strict failures and unknown historical N08 cause.
- Complete all remaining mandatory PB05 backend/capability/continuity and applicable returning-user scenarios before acceptance; preserve original red and fixture failures.
- Long S/W/E endurance is DEFERRED_BY_USER, not waived or passed; continue bounded functional work only.
