Findings:

- **PB07-D01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** OpenClaw public CLI fallback adds --message fullPrompt to buildCliArgs containing --message-file; installed CLI rejects both and the named file is never prepared.
- **PB07-D02 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Reachable Stop during custom OpenClaw prompt/reconnect preparation has no current-request check before spawn; finalizer targets mutable session.process and unqualified clearProcess(panel).
- **PB07-D03 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Production JSON5 ESM exports do not provide the named parse import; gateway config token and account label parsing silently fell back despite source tests passing.
- **PB07-H01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Initial mock setting used a prefixed key, sending one synthetic connect to default localhost gateway; next attempt asserted socket closure before the close event.
- **PB07-D04 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Token/account configuration was hardcoded to the default home path; credential detection missed the current agent override and did not expand paths, so relocated installations could select the wrong token or appear unauthenticated.
- **PB07-D05 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Any nonempty object or array in the credential store, and whitespace environment variables, could report OpenClaw authenticated without valid credential material. Static token expiry was ignored.
- **PB07-D06 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Gateway tokens in configuration were omitted from readiness while environment gateway tokens counted even with gateway transport disabled. The gateway captured its token at provider construction, so setup/rotation/removal did not take effect on reconnect. Blank tokens also masked environment fallback.
- **PB07-D07 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Credential checks always used main or deprecated SDK directory overrides and read only JSON. Current native execution resolves the configured default agent and reads SQLite, causing false negatives; stale JSON could also report revoked credentials as ready.
- **PB07-H02 (FIXED_LOCAL):** The package gate classified node:sqlite as an absent sqlite npm dependency even though the reader worked in the pinned editor runtime.
- **PB07-D08 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Relative OpenClaw config/state/home/agent paths were checked from the extension cwd while the CLI ran from the workspace, causing false ready/not-ready states and incorrect account labels.
- **PB07-D09 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** An empty workspace folder array was treated as containing a first folder, preventing the CLI from starting.
- **PB07-D10 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Enhancement ignored the gateway-disable setting and otherwise invoked an agent CLI without a required session selector; it also lacked text-only controls.
- **PB07-D11 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Parsing stdout by chunk could lose valid output or replace a draft with raw JSON, broken UTF-8 or diagnostic text. Gateway errors could accept partial text or replay a failed request locally.
- **PB07-D12 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Enhancement had no local output/deadline bound and its children were not stopped by provider disposal.
- **PB07-D13 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Raw stderr chunks, structured errors and spawn Error objects could expose credentials or arguments; rotation and Stop could remove the credential context needed for filtering.
- **PB07-D14 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Tagged failed tool completions could appear completed or disappear; structured terminal errors could be duplicated, and failed plain stdout was emitted as response text.
- **PB07-D15 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Decoding each buffer independently corrupted multibyte characters in replies and credentials; altered credentials no longer matched the redaction snapshot.
- **PB07-D16 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Handshake and connection failures, final/stream errors, raw payload logs and provider catch errors could disclose credentials or private request/routing data, including after token rotation.
- **PB07-D17 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Failed tagged tools/steps could appear running or successful. Standalone/step structured failures could expose unfiltered credential and extra request metadata.
- **PB07-D18 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Gateway did not advertise or parse native tool events. Legacy gateway and standalone CLI results could lose identity/input and retain completed tool state.
- **PB07-D19 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Nested native final-only replies were lost; successful RPC envelopes could hide timeout or failed agent runs. Native lifecycle failures were ignored, and public gateway auth errors did not trigger sign-in recovery.
- **PB07-D20 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Gateway dropped native usage metadata; CLI dropped cached-token buckets and final metadata after streamed text. Capability metadata suppressed usage, and no separate latest-context measurement was available.
- **PB07-D21 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native reported provider/model was discarded and gateway context window was lost, obscuring which backend model answered.
- **PB07-D22 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native CLI payloads marked isError were displayed as assistant replies; model attribution was absent on those failures.
- **PB07-D23 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native stream:error was ignored, so explicit event gaps could hang or appear successful; repeated sequenced events duplicated text/tool cards.
- **PB07-D24 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Cancelled handshakes waited for timeout, URL changes could retain the old attempt, shutdown left active requests pending, and a replacement socket could inherit readiness before authentication.
- **PB07-D25 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Per-connection broadcast sequence gaps were ignored, despite native drops advancing the counter; duplicate outer frames replayed their content.
- **PB07-D26 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Stop before acknowledgment omitted runId and could cancel another owner in the same native session.
- **PB07-D27 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Concurrent Stop sent duplicate aborts; repeated timestamp/random values produced duplicate native request identities.
- **PB07-D28 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Assistant/thinking events without delta were ignored, so snapshots or their final suffixes disappeared after earlier text had streamed.
- **PB07-D29 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native replacement events previously left stale text looking final or concatenated replacement text. Revision ranges now update live/history content and reach append-only consumers through an announced gateway finalization path.
- **PB07-H03 (TEST_FIXED):** The enhancement fixture published readiness before installing its SIGTERM handler; a concurrent test could kill the supposedly stubborn child before that handler existed.
- **PB07-D30 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native commentary was emitted as answer text and suppressed the final envelope; native item progress was silently ignored.
- **PB07-D31 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Final payload extraction included commentary and dropped structured signed text blocks; provisional reconciliation could include commentary or miss top-level payloads.
- **PB07-D32 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Intermediate agent acknowledgement handling also held ordinary RPC responses and explicit failures with ack-like payload status until timeout.
- **PB07-D33 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native in-flight agent responses looked like empty completed replies; RPC-ok aborted delegations looked accepted. Some terminal abort and legacy top-level error metadata was ignored.
- **PB07-D34 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Gateway token string references were sent literally, while env SecretRef objects were ignored, causing authentication failure or false unavailable readiness.
- **PB07-D35 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** A nonempty delta overrode its full snapshot, losing visible text after missing events and corrupting revision offsets. Snapshot-only extensions could duplicate raw trailing whitespace; ambiguous stable message changes were silently appended.
- **PB07-D36 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Local readiness ignored non-Anthropic backend environment credentials and accepted an unrelated Anthropic key even with a different explicitly configured backend.
- **PB07-D37 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** OpenClaw local readiness ignored provider-config API keys and complete native local no-auth configurations, even though the installed CLI could use the same configuration for inference.
- **PB07-D38 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Any usable stored profile could qualify an unrelated selected backend or rescue an explicitly missing/expired/incompatible profile. Persisted runtime order was ignored.
- **PB07-D39 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Mysti recognizes a JSON-only auth-profiles store as a legacy readiness hint, while installed OpenClaw2026.6.34 ignored the same owned store and failed authentication. Current-version and legacy store eligibility need to follow the selected CLI capability.
- **PB07-D40 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** JSON readiness merged retired auth.json entries into a valid auth-profiles.json envelope and ignored auth-state.json order. Removed profiles or expired ordered selections could incorrectly appear ready, while a recovered runtime selection could remain unready.
- **PB07-D41 (OPEN_NATIVE_LIMITATION):** Installed OpenClaw2026.6.34 can take about60seconds to answer the next same-session request after Stop before the first model token. Its lane-timeout final can precede underlying cleanup. The unchanged Mysti implementation eventually recovers; no permanent Mysti deadlock or effective local fix was established.
- **PB07-D42 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** A native model401echoing its configured API key exposed the value in Mysti gateway errors/logs. CLI diagnostic snapshots covered environment secrets but omitted configured backend keys.
- **PB07-D43 (OPEN_NATIVE_LIMITATION):** A controlled model stream ending after a content delta without finish_reason or [DONE] is reported by installed OpenClaw2026.6.34 as status=ok with stop/stop completion. Mysti displays the partial answer as completed because the native envelope gives no failure marker.
- **PB07-D44 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native gateway FailoverError:401 reaches Mysti as a generic error instead of auth_error, while localCLI401 correctly requests authentication. Both visibly terminate and recover; gateway auth guidance/classification needs a focused fix.
- **PB07-D45 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** A key stored only in native SQLite auth profiles appeared verbatim in gateway401diagnostics; config/env-only redaction omitted locally stored credentials.
- **PB07-D46 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** After an actual native CLI worker crash, Mysti displayed routine stderr progress logs as the error and omitted the nonzero process exit reason.
- **PB07-D47 (OPEN_NATIVE_LIMITATION):** After Mysti reported a gateway crash, installed OpenClaw2026.6.34 restarted the interrupted inference without another Mysti agent request. A slower restart produced three model requests for two Mysti requests; native automatic recovery can outlive the foreground failure.
- **PB07-D48 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** OpenClaw custom hybrid stdout reader bypassed the shared inactivity watchdog, leaving a silent open pipe unbounded before waitForProcess could run.
- **PB07-D49 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** When stderr progress extended inactivity, the shared CLI reader called iterator.next again while the old read was still pending. The next stdout reply was consumed by the abandoned timeout race and lost.

Still required:

- Real inference blocked at current local-CLI auth readiness: selected main-agent SQLite credential store absent; legacy JSON token is not eligible and must not be silently resurrected. Obtain current native sign-in or explicitly configured ready backend, then complete required 25-turn F1/F2 actual model coding tasks. Actual user gateway remains untouched/unqualified. Controlled model/context fault recovery passed separately; successful compaction, real account behavior, packaged-editor restart/upgrade and wider environments remain required.
- PB07-D41 native slow Stop cleanup and PB07-D43 false completion on truncated model streams and PB07-D47 background inference recovery after gateway restart remain open. Native interrupted-prompt merging does not establish exactly-once tool effects across crashes. Real-account inference, remote/externally refreshed credentials, config overlays and unsupported secret/runtime integrations remain unqualified; controlled transport fixtures do not certify model task quality.
- Recoverable snapshot-plus-delta text is covered by PB07-D35. Targeted tool/item losses, whole lost messages, prefix-identical lost message boundaries and missing tails without a later snapshot remain unobservable through text reconciliation alone.
- Resume remaining PB-07 obligations after current auth/backend readiness changes. Independent PB-08 testing proceeds meanwhile; no PB-07 completion is claimed. Endurance stays DEFERRED_BY_USER.

### PB-08 — OpenCode

**Status: IN_PROGRESS.** Fixed PB08-D08 local CLI Stop leaving shell tools in separate process groups. Installed macOS shell/sleep exited 48 ms after Stop; no delayed write over 12.5 seconds, recovery and owned-process cleanup passed. 366 regressions/36 files and typecheck/targeted lint passed. Reproducible sandboxed native runner and sanitized evidence are retained in the repository. Earlier PB08-D07 remote cleanup remains verified only while its provider instance survives. Real inference, remaining native faults/context/concurrency, other platforms and user-deferred endurance remain incomplete; PB-08 stays IN_PROGRESS.

Findings:

- **PB08-D01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** CLI Plan was advertised as Read Only although native Plan allows shell and agent/resumed-session rules can override global restrictions.
- **PB08-D02 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Remote discovery downloaded the full 5.8 MB catalog and shared one seven-second deadline across health and workspace/provider initialization, causing native readiness timeouts.
- **PB08-D03 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** When the final HTTP reply arrived before a terminal SSE tool event, Mysti discarded final tool state. A native rejected write left a running tool and reported no response.
- **PB08-D04 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Installed OpenCode 1.18.29 emits terminal tool_use events containing a native ToolPart; Mysti read flat fields, showed an unnamed running tool and never completed its card. Top-level reasoning was also ignored.
- **PB08-D05 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** OpenCode CLI usage retained only the last step and dropped disjoint cached-input buckets; summing steps also requires distinct latest-call context usage to avoid an inflated context meter.
- **PB08-D06 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** Native unknown-model HTTP 500 incorrectly suggested a server-password problem; native server death exposed TypeError: terminated.
- **PB08-D07 (FIXED_LOCAL_AND_PACKAGED):** A session created for a crashed remote request remains after the same server restarts and a subsequent Mysti turn succeeds. Abort/delete attempts fail while offline and are not retried. Reproduced in both native runs.
- **PB08-D08 (FIXED_NATIVE_VERIFIED):** Stop killed the local OpenCode CLI while its shell tool and sleep survived in an independent POSIX process group. Earlier model-pending cancellation checks did not cover running tools.

Still required:

- Installed-native macOS CLI cancellation during a running shell tool is now verified with a scripted loopback model. Qualify remaining terminal/authentication failures and larger history/context cases; other-platform running-tool cancellation remains unqualified.
- Qualify remaining native remote directory/endpoint changes, context limits, abrupt network reset and concurrent panels. Cold startup still needs outer discovery-service/model-registry qualification.
- Deferred session cleanup now works across a server restart while the provider instance survives. Pending cleanup is not persisted across extension reloads; durability across editor shutdown/reload remains outside this acceptance.
- Real account F1/F2 inference and WSL/Docker/cross-platform network acceptance remain pending. Scripted responses prove mechanics only; endurance remains DEFERRED_BY_USER.

- **PB11-D01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Stale or unknown RPC responses ended a later public turn and hid its reasoning/final answer.
- **PB11-D02 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Incomplete clean EOF appeared successful and setup failure could be replaced by diagnostic fallback.
- **PB11-D03 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Malformed current session/prompt replies left a request unsettled with a reusable invalid connection.
- **PB11-D04 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Saved native allow rules and default approvals can execute without ACP permission callbacks; Plan does not veto commands/delegation. Current Mysti constrained-mode claims are not enforceable through this adapter.
- **PB11-D05 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Explicit model selection only injects ANTHROPIC_MODEL; installed ACP exposes a session model setter and returns config options that the adapter ignores. Native model selection remains unqualified.
- **PB11-D06 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Mode tooltip and visible capability hint suggested Read Only for five providers whose capabilities prohibit it.

Still required:

- Native account/backend, reasoning/files/images and resumed workflow require separate functional acceptance.
- Broader malformed initialize handling remains separate from these bounded fixes.
- Endurance remains DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **PB13-D01 (FIXED_LOCAL):** An older response return or timeout could cancel a newer same-panel request and leave its own HTTP source open.
- **PB13-D02 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Overlapping same-panel responses could steal or lose each other’s usage totals.
- **PB13-D03 (FIXED_LOCAL):** No-panel cancellation, clear and provider disposal could leave current HTTP panel requests running.
- **PB13-D04 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Ollama emitted buffered output after Stop, ignored Stop during its final context lookup, and retained chat response reader locks.
- **PB13-D05 (FIXED_LOCAL):** A provider-wide thinking string could reject legacy non-thinking Ollama models or request levels unsupported by the selected model.
- **PB13-D06 (FIXED_LOCAL):** Ollama could report success after streamed server errors or terminal-free EOF, discard a final non-newline JSON record and lose text/usage co-located with a tool proposal.

Still required:

- Exact model context extraction and native cancellation remain unqualified after four closed failed attempts; no further native retry is planned.
- Investigate the separately reported placeholder rendering issue, then qualify PB05 packaged startup-probe capture with zero model requests; that check is queued and has not run.
- Retain scoped R3/R4 transport, public history and separate offline persistence evidence without promoting native acceptance.
- Endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **EW01-D01 (FIXED):** Folder work and picker/drop batches stop after context clear, panel disposal or owner/navigation changes; Explorer and dropped folders reach folder expansion.
- **EW01-D02 (FIXED):** Folder scans preserve the first-20 selection for complete listings within the work budget and avoid later directory visits once that prefix is filled. Incomplete oversized listings disclose partial selection; the former sparse-prefix gap is addressed by EW01-D06.
- **EW01-D03 (FIXED):** Folder additions report reaching the 20-file candidate limit without claiming knowledge of an omitted 21st file. Duplicate and unreadable candidates continue to count toward that prefix; notices guide additional individual file additions.
- **EW01-D04 (FIXED):** An unreadable directory branch no longer ends the parent walk. Readable siblings and valid files remain available, while failed files and directories are identified in current-owner feedback.
- **EW01-D05 (FIXED):** Explorer commands and URI/path drops share feedback for empty or failed additions. Repeated folders are no longer falsely described as unreadable, and late failures cannot notify or recreate disposed context.
- **EW01-D06 (FIXED):** Directory enumeration now uses a 32-entry native buffer, a 200-directory attempt limit and a 10,000-entry limit per folder addition. Complete listings retain UTF-8 filename order; interrupted listings report partial selection. Handles close before recursion and after stale open/read completion, Clear, navigation, disposal or iteration failure.
- **EW01-D07 (FIXED):** Context file additions now use bounded regular-file reads and strict UTF-8/no-NUL validation. Files over 10 MiB, including growth after metadata capture, are rejected with specific size/text feedback. Mixed folders retain valid files; late read/close results cannot populate or warn a cleared/replaced chat.

Still required:

- Live-send freshness after disk edits/deletion/replacement is the next source-based candidate under EW-02; add/restore reader checks alone do not prove dispatch uses current bytes.
- Native Explorer/editor interaction, other platforms and remaining recursive-link, normalization and permission-change scenarios are pending.
- Per-file bytes and directory work are bounded; aggregate context/prompt size, OS syscall latency and full heap/elapsed-time behavior are not certified.
- Required sustained/resource profiles remain incomplete; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Actual installed provider/model acceptance, image/clipboard/platform matrix, other lifecycle faults and prescribed profiles remain incomplete. Findings:

- **EW02-D01 (FIXED):** Late context restoration could undo Clear, recreate disposed panel state, or replace newer selections/files/restores. Newer user actions now invalidate stale restores.
- **EW02-D02 (FIXED):** Successful ordinary restoration preserves bucket identity so explicit additions opened during restore are accepted and cancelled pickers preserve saved context.
- **EW02-D03 (FIXED):** Saved file restoration and the refresh helper no longer perform unbounded or lossy UTF-8 reads. Rejected files are pruned while valid files and selection snapshots survive; startup restoration reports the rejection reason to the original current panel.
- **EW02-D04 (FIXED):** Restoration diagnostics normalize damaged saved path fields so warning formatting cannot throw and interrupt valid sibling restoration. Late restore failure notifications are suppressed after context or conversation ownership changes.
- **EW02-D05 (FIXED):** Ordinary follow-up requests reused cached contents after file edits, deletion or rejected replacement. A per-request bounded refresh now rereads live enabled panel files, preserves prior message/selection/second-opinion snapshots, atomically updates the current context, and refuses stale preparation after Clear/remove, Stop, navigation, close or supersession.
- **EW02-D06 (FIXED):** The composer cleared submitted text and attachments before the host recorded the question; cancellation during context/settings/compaction preparation left no visible recovery route. Correlated host receipts now retain unrecorded submissions for explicit restoration in their original conversation, without overwriting newer drafts or retrying automatically. Recovery clicks are contained so slash-menu dismissal cannot erase a restored session command.

Still required:

- Extend native coverage beyond normal changed/deleted files and two editor tabs: rejected replacement contents, delayed read ownership, startup restoration and panel close/reload. Four current-package loopback native cases passed; no full feature acceptance claim.
- Complete native multi-panel/editor/platform and remaining feature-plan fault cases; historical full-suite/native results are not fresh evidence for this package.
- Required sustained/resource profiles remain incomplete; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

### EW-03 — Automatic active-file and selection context

**Status: PARTIAL.** Automatic editor context and explicit references now reach final provider sends, including every user-started session shape and later rounds. Session Stop/navigation/successor and saved-context isolation are verified. Selection labels use one-based lines with exclusive-end handling. 1,706 targeted tests across146 files and local VSIX 1647bf7d pass. Saved manual-only second-opinion behavior is retained by contract. Native acceptance remains pending; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **EW03-D01 (FIXED):** Direct provider composer messages now receive the current selection or unsaved document, using a panel-owned visible editor across focus changes, live Auto disabling and explicit same-file context precedence.
- **EW03-D02 (FIXED):** Obsolete composer preparation cannot dispatch after panel replacement or conversation/navigation change; abandoned preparation releases busy state while active responses still finalize.
- **EW03-D03 (FIXED):** Explicit context and request-local automatic context now reach coordinator, collaboration, delegation, custom provider transports and every user-started session phase. Internal sessions use supplied context without acquiring the current editor.
- **EW03-D04 (FIXED):** Live private context factories preserve the original capture through model queues/backoff/fallback, collaborator readiness/retries/questions, and shared CLI instructions/attachment waits; canonical prompts, authority and nonces are preserved.
- **EW03-D05 (FIXED):** Revoked preparation and superseded orchestration release their own busy state without empty assistant persistence, false stream errors or successor UI cancellation; coordinator nudges and run history remain intact.
- **EW03-D06 (FIXED):** Custom providers resolve input after final setup waits and reject obsolete requests without fallback; OpenCode suppresses buffered output after Stop and OpenRouter retains completion ownership through metadata lookup.
- **EW03-D07 (FIXED):** Selection prompt and browser labels now display one-based line ranges and exclude an empty exclusive final line; raw stored coordinates/content are preserved with an explicit legacy fallback.
- **EW03-D08 (FIXED):** Session Stop/navigation prevents later-phase dispatch and releases only owned busy state. Private context copies prevent saved-message edits from rewriting a pending session request.

Still required:

- Native editor focus/event ordering and real-provider acceptance remain unqualified. Preserve the packaged-editor sandbox startup failure and Ollama exact-answer failure; retry only after relevant conditions change.
- Endurance remains DEFERRED_BY_USER; no large paid request campaign is authorized.

### EW-04 — @file context resolution and referenced-file transport

**Status: PARTIAL.** File identity, quoted syntax, binding, physical containment, shorthand uniqueness, bounded content reading, dispatch and cancellation are locally verified. Unknown typed paths, dotted names and dotfiles now give feedback before send or queue capture, preserving draft, attachments and Context. Queue dispatch revalidates references; rejected references retain queued work and do not release a Stop hold. Plain @words/code/email remain text. 2,665 targeted checks across 183 files and local VSIX 9791af6b pass. Native and sustained-profile acceptance remain; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER. File-reference prompt boundaries are now locally verified across ordinary, collaborator, Brainstorm, Mysti and orchestration routes; quoted filename escapes stay literal in assigned tasks. Fresh qualification on November 2: 1,312 checks across 107 files and local package ca839809. Earlier out-test artifacts were absent at resume; this fresh package is not an inherited full-suite or native release.

Structural tests do not establish model resistance. | [1](../out-test/file-mention-prompt-20261102/result.json), [2](../out-test/file-mention-prompt-20261102/focused-r3-receipts.jsonl), [3](../out-test/file-mention-prompt-20261102/combined-r4.json) |
| EW04-PROMPT-PACKAGE-20261102 | TARGETED_LOCAL_PACKAGE | PASS | Fresh local ca839809; typecheck, lint, package prepublish release build and package shape/parity pass. Archive CRC and root bundle match verified with current source-map joins. No ordinary-profile installation, actual provider request, full-suite rerun or native acceptance. | [1](../out-test/file-mention-prompt-20261102/result.json), [2](../out-test/file-mention-prompt-20261102/focused-r3-receipts.jsonl) |

Findings:

- **EW04-D01 (FIXED):** Selecting one of several same-basename files now inserts its exact JSON-quoted path; queued sends and cache reorder preserve selection. Ambiguous typed names and stale quoted paths refuse before clearing the draft.
- **EW04-D02 (FIXED):** Quoted spaces, Unicode, quotes and backticks preserve exact filename bytes without generating agent assignments. Browser/host code and token boundaries agree, including escaped and astral email-like text. Suggestion markup and attributes are escaped.
- **EW04-D03 (FIXED):** File mentions now resolve once before Mysti inline/background/orchestration dispatch and before brainstorm analysis/synthesis; final requests retain exact selected bytes and warnings while saved manual context stays unchanged.
- **EW04-D04 (FIXED):** Revoked file reads discard results and stop subsequent reads. Internal Mysti navigation releases owned lifecycle state; brainstorm respects compaction Stop, pending submission ownership, predecessor retirement and successor-safe cleanup.
- **EW04-D05 (FIXED):** Host binds supplied file metadata to complete visible tokens before reads/turn takeover, clones references across waits, and revalidates second opinions against saved source. Invalid initial sends preserve active/pending responses; valid next sends recover.
- **EW04-D06 (FIXED):** File mentions require a regular physical target beneath a declared workspace root. Canonical path/root and same-handle file identity checks reject observed escapes or retargeting before reading. Workspace-list changes discard all collected reference bytes; allowed links across open roots remain supported.
- **EW04-D07 (FIXED):** Ordinary/explicit file preparation and internal sends capture panel, conversation and navigation ownership. Obsolete preparation stops reads/dispatch and settles its own UI state; stale generator entry cannot cancel a successor.
- **EW04-D08 (FIXED):** Host-derived token descriptors require typed shorthand to identify one canonical file in a fresh bounded editor search catalogue. Ambiguity and incomplete lookup omit the reference with a warning. Absolute selection stays explicit; relative-only metadata resolves a unique second-root target. The reader verifies discovery identity before opening.
- **EW04-D09 (FIXED):** A workspace-folder event permanently revokes the in-flight file-context snapshot even when the folder array is restored before polling. All collected reference bytes are discarded, late query results cannot be dispatched, and the temporary subscription is disposed.
- **EW04-D10 (FIXED):** Raw NUL bytes cause a mentioned file to be omitted as binary with an explanation; no partial content reaches the provider, and valid references/manual Context continue. The heuristic is independent of file extension and does not claim to classify every binary format.
- **EW04-D11 (FIXED):** A per-file 5,000,000-byte limit is enforced both by metadata and actual bounded reads through the verified handle, including growth after stat. UTF-8 chunks decode once, cancellation suppresses partial context, and handles close on every terminal path. This introduces no aggregate-turn cap.
- **EW04-D12 (FIXED):** Unresolved explicit file syntax now reports current-file-list feedback before composer send or busy queue capture, preserving draft/attachments/Context. Short bare syntax requests quoted selection without changing host grammar. Queue dispatch revalidates current references and preserves both queued item and active draft when rejected. No filesystem absence claim or new host reads.
- **EW04-D13 (FIXED):** File contents and path/language/range metadata remain within explicit reference-data boundaries across provider and Mysti reference routes. Checked markers preserve supplied bytes on the shared formatter; existing direct Mysti nonce redaction and context caps remain. File metadata is enclosed in collaborator/orchestrator fences and planner/path-only manifests.
- **EW04-D14 (FIXED):** Explicit collaborator assignment planning keeps quoted filename spelling and escapes instead of decoding filename newlines into the task. Bare-file assignment identity and agent routing remain intact.

Still required:

- Native editor search/cache/watcher/restart, persisted references after renames/upgrades and actual provider acceptance remain pending for full EW04.
- Structural prompt tests do not prove how a real model will interpret instruction-like file data. Existing truncation and nonce redaction remain intentional on the relevant Mysti routes.
- Required sustained W/E/P profiles remain incomplete; endurance is DEFERRED_BY_USER.

### EW-05 — File/image attachment picker and capability handling

**Status: PARTIAL.** Nov 3 bounded native validation of unchanged package 0b542da0: four LocalAI loopback cases cover changed/deleted file refresh, original message snapshots, editor-tab context isolation and cancelled-text recovery. Three additional native cases cover real file picking and attachment recovery through the packaged Claude adapter with a synthetic CLI: selected nonempty/empty bytes survive source modification/deletion; Stop during Mysti manual compaction preserves the submission alongside newer drafts and attachments; navigation retains recovery in the original conversation. Four attachment snapshots were cleaned and all seven fixture processes exited. Existing 716 targeted/compatibility checks remain prior evidence. Actual installed provider/model acceptance, image/clipboard/platform matrix, other lifecycle faults and prescribed profiles remain incomplete. No ordinary-profile installation; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **EW05-D01 (FIXED):** A file growing after picker metadata no longer bypasses the 10 MiB limit. The opened handle and actual bounded reads enforce it; oversized data is never staged partially, and the reader closes on every terminal path. This is a per-file bound, not a total batch cap.
- **EW05-D02 (FIXED):** Attachment size uses captured buffer length rather than stale stat metadata, including growth and shrinkage within the limit. Accepted binary bytes and a maximum-sized CLI preparation snapshot retain their hashes.
- **EW05-D03 (FIXED):** CLI attachment preparation distinguishes an empty captured payload from missing payload data. Empty files retain zero-byte contents after source modification/deletion, receive independent temporary paths, and clean up without deleting intentional path-only originals.
- **EW05-D04 (FIXED):** Composer attachment previews escape file names, IDs and data URL attribute values. A crafted filename can no longer introduce elements/event handlers through that preview; live/restored message rendering remains a separate acceptance item.
- **EW05-D05 (FIXED):** Message image thumbnails escape the entire data URL attribute. Malformed MIME/base64 strings can no longer create extra HTML elements or event handlers through that field. The actual host/provider-bound bytes remain unchanged; normal PNG/SVG display and persisted/imported labels are verified.

Still required:

- Native file picker and selected nonempty/empty byte snapshots pass through the synthetic Claude CLI adapter on macOS. Actual model/installed CLI byte-and-answer acceptance, native images/clipboard/Explorer and remaining provider/platform capability matrices are pending.
- Semantic MIME/corrupt-file disposition and remaining URI/target routes need functional qualification.
- Required sustained and resource profiles remain incomplete; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.

Findings:

- **EW06-D01 (FIXED):** Pending paste/drop reads are cancelled on conversation changes, initial-state rebinding and page disposal. Removed or superseded readers cannot append bytes or show stale errors, even when a callback was already queued.
- **EW06-D02 (FIXED):** Pending reads reserve slots in the same ten-attachment limit as completed files and picker results. Failure/removal releases the slot; mixed batches retain valid items.
- **EW06-D03 (FIXED):** Loading previews expose cancellation and keep their draft unsent/unqueued until reads finish or are removed. Rejected slash-menu sends retain draft text. Already queued messages and second opinions keep independent ownership while draft files load.
- **EW06-D04 (FIXED):** Paste read errors now show per-file feedback. Synchronous read failures and malformed results release callbacks/slots and leave other attachments available.
- **EW06-D05 (FIXED):** Dropped URI strings reach host-native fileURLToPath validation intact. Localhost/uppercase schemes and escaped filenames resolve correctly; unsupported schemes, malformed escapes, raw controls, separators, NUL and query/fragment suffixes are rejected before filesystem access. Both supported MIME formats are considered.
- **EW06-D06 (FIXED):** A Context-target drop resets the shared document overlay and nested drag count, preventing a persistent overlay after propagation stops at that target.
- **EW06-D07 (FIXED):** Mixed drop batches retain valid files and report failures; repeated files do not produce false unreadable warnings. Empty Context drops and fallback to message attachments are explained, as are URI-only composer drops. Stale batches do not warn a replacement conversation.

Still required:

- Native OS clipboard, Explorer drag/drop and platform matrices remain pending; macOS file URL checks do not certify Windows UNC behavior.
- Folder-limit and nested-read feedback is qualified under EW-01 (out-test/context-folder-20261102/result.json). Unsupported/mismatched MIME and corrupt-file disposition still need functional qualification; message attribute escaping is separately recorded in EW-05.
- Required sustained and heap/resource profiles remain incomplete; endurance DEFERRED_BY_USER.
